assistant: VERDICT: REJECT\n\n1. **`src/troupe/remote.py:194-208` and `:222-241`: connecting is not covered by the 10 s total deadline, so NFR-004 is broken.** `_connection` reads `self._deadline.remaining()` once, when the `HTTPConnection`/`HTTPSConnection` object is created. After that, the stdlib uses that fixed number for everything up to the first read:\n   - **Each address gets the full timeout.** `socket.create_connection` tries every address `getaddrinfo` returns, and gives each one the full timeout. A host with two addresses that both drop packets takes about 20 s; with four, about 40 s. That is a common case: dual-stack A plus AAAA records, or a load balancer with several A records behind a firewall that drops packets. I reproduced it read-only. A listener with a full backlog (it never answers the SYN) was returned twice by a patched `getaddrinfo` (`return r + r`). `fetch(Url("http://dualstack.example:<port>/x.yaml"))` raised `cannot fetch ...: timed out after 10 s` after **20.0 s** of real time.\n   - **The TLS handshake gets the stale value too.** `HTTPSConnection.connect` does the TCP connect and then calls `wrap_socket`. The handshake runs with the socket timeout set when the connection was created, not with the time left. So a slow or failed connect followed by a stalled handshake can also run to about 2× the deadline. Example: the first address drops packets, the second accepts but never finishes TLS.\n   - **The docstring is therefore wrong.** `_Deadline` says "every connection is created with the *remaining* time … No blocking call can therefore wait past the deadline". The first claim is true; the second is not.\n\n   Required:\n   - Give every connect attempt the time left. Before each address is tried, call `deadline.remaining()`. For example, write your own `create_connection` loop (assigned to `connection._create_connection`, or done in an overridden `connect`) that calls `remaining()` per address and stops with `TimeoutError` once time runs out.\n   - In an `HTTPSConnection` subclass, call `self.sock.settimeout(deadline.remaining())` after the TCP connect and before `wrap_socket`. Do the same before the tunnel and proxy `CONNECT` step if you touch `connect`.\n   - Add a test proving it, in the same style as the hang/trickle tests: `elapsed < 12.0`. A listener with a full backlog plus a patched `getaddrinfo` that returns its address twice works offline and deterministically. It is the reproduction above.\n   - Correct the `_Deadline` docstring so it matches what the code really does.\n\n**Non-blocking notes**\n\n- **DNS lookup has no time limit.** `getaddrinfo` cannot be given a timeout and depends on the resolver configuration. Either run the lookup in a daemon thread and wait on it for the time left, or state the limit plainly in the module docstring so the claim stops being absolute.\n- **Possible trailing space in the HTTP error.** `HTTP {err.code} {err.reason}` ends with a space when the server sends a status line with no reason phrase. Consider `.rstrip()`.\n- **The test server does the TLS handshake in its accept loop.** `RemoteServer(tls=...)` wraps the listening socket, so every handshake runs inside `accept()` on the `serve_forever` thread. A client that connects and never handshakes would block the server and delay `close()`. This is fine for the current test, where the client fails verification and drops the connection straight away. Keep it in mind if later WPs use `https_remote` more widely.\n\nEverything else checked out as specified:\n- **`Url`/`Location`:** frozen; `__str__` returns the text unchanged; no `troupe` imports.\n- **`FetchError`:** carries `url`/`reason`; message `cannot fetch {url}: {reason}`; subclass of `LoadError` with exit 4; `errors.py` stays free of `troupe` imports.\n- **Opener:** a minimal handler set. No auth handler, no cookie processor, no `ftp`/`file` handlers.\n- **TLS:** `ssl.create_default_context()`, and verification is never weakened, tests included.\n- **Redirects:** the check sits in `http_error_30x` and catches `file:`/`ftp:` before urllib does.\n- **Trickle defence:** the `_DeadlineReader`/`BufferedReader` swap works. `BufferedReader.readinto` loops over raw reads and each one re-arms the socket timeout. The deadline therefore holds for the status line, the headers and a trickling body.\n- **Errors and body:** every exception is turned into `FetchError`; decoding is strict UTF-8 with the BOM kept and `charset` ignored.\n- **Network imports:** `remote.py` is the only module under `src` that imports `urllib`/`ssl`/`socket`/`http.client`.\n- **Test server:** request log is locked and covers every method; `hang`/`trickle` routes stop on the shared event; daemon threads.\n- **Fixtures:** `https_remote` skips with the required reason when `openssl` is missing. `run_cli` and the `WatchProcess` environment set `NO_PROXY`/`no_proxy` before the caller's `env`.\n- **Tests:** names match what they assert; `tests/unit/test_remote_fetch.py` passes (20 tests, 27.7 s).\n- **Scope:** `src` and `tests` changes stay within the owned files.\n\nRelevant files:\n- /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-a/src/troupe/remote.py\n- /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-a/tests/unit/test_remote_fetch.py\n- /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-a/tests/remote_server.py

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /tmp && openssl req -x509 -newkey rsa:2048 -nodes -days 1 -subj /CN=localhost -addext \"subjectAltName=DNS:localhost\" -keyout /tmp/pk.key -out /tmp/pk.crt 2>/dev/null && cat > /tmp/tunnelcheck.py <<'EOF'\nimport sys, os, socket, ssl, threading\nsys.path.insert(0, \"/work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-a/tests\")\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\nfrom troupe.remote import fetch\nfrom troupe.location import Url\nctx = ssl.SSLContext(ssl.PROTOCOL_TLS_SERVER); ctx.load_cert_chain(\"/tmp/pk.crt\", \"/tmp/pk.key\")\ns = RemoteServer(tls=ctx); s.serve(\"/x.yaml\", \"via tunnel\\n\")\nport = int(s.base.rsplit(\":\", 1)[1])\nlst = socket.create_server((\"127.0.0.1\", 0)); seen = []\ndef pipe(a, b):\n    while d := a.recv(65536): b.sendall(d)\n    b.close()\ndef proxy():\n    c, _ = lst.accept(); head = b\"\"\n    while b\"\\r\\n\\r\\n\" not in head: head += c.recv(1)\n    seen.append(head.split(b\"\\r\\n\")[0])\n    host, p = head.split()[1].decode().rsplit(\":\", 1)\n    u = socket.create_connection((\"127.0.0.1\", int(p)))\n    c.sendall(b\"HTTP/1.1 200 OK\\r\\n\\r\\n\")\n    threading.Thread(target=pipe, args=(u, c), daemon=True).start(); pipe(c, u)\nthreading.Thread(target=proxy, daemon=True).start()\nos.environ[\"https_proxy\"] = f\"http://127.0.0.1:{lst.getsockname()[1]}\"\nprint(repr(fetch(Url(f\"https://localhost:{port}/x.yaml\"))), seen, s.requests())\nEOF\ncd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-a && SSL_CERT_FILE=/tmp/pk.crt uv run python /tmp/tunnelcheck.py", "description": "Ad hoc HTTPS-through-CONNECT-proxy check"}

Connections now resolve in a daemon thread\nwaited on for the remaining time, connect per address with the timeout re-armed each attempt,\nre-arm before every send (request and proxy CONNECT) and before the TLS handshake. The HTTP\nerror reason is rstripped so a status line without a reason phrase leaves no trailing space.\nAdds offline tests for both timeouts and the bare status line.\nEOF\ngit log --oneline -1 | cat; git status --short", "description": "Clean caches and commit"}

out = f\"OK {r!r}\"\n    except FetchError as e: out = f\"FetchError {e}\"\n    except BaseException as e: out = f\"LEAK {type(e).__name__}: {e}\"\n    el = time.monotonic() - t\n    teardown()\n    flag = \"PASS\" if el < T + 1.5 else \"FAIL\"\n    print(f\"[{flag}] {name}: {el:.2f}s -> {out}\", flush=True)\n\ndef full_backlog():\n    l = socket.socket(); tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"The include rule: the one place that decides what an include string is and what it points at\n(TROUPE-007; research R-3, R-8, R-9).\n\n- ``include_kind`` tells a ``file:`` include from a remote (``https://``/``http://``) one.\n- ``resolve_target`` turns an include string into the ``Location`` it names (spec S-1).\n- ``is_yaml_target`` decides YAML or text by the suffix of the path or URL path (S-2).\n- ``reads_as_include`` is the literal-versus-include rule for scalar strings (S-7, S-10).\n- ``path_steps`` gives the key/index kind of each PATH segment, for ``Schema.uri_at``.\n- ``mark_literals`` decides which VALUE strings ``set`` writes ``!literal`` and refuses\n  ``--include`` on a ``format: uri`` path (S-11, S-12, S-13).\n\nThis module imports nothing that imports ``troupe.loader``, so the loader can import it.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom collections.abc import Sequence\nfrom pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath\nfrom typing import Final, Literal\nfrom urllib.parse import urljoin, urlsplit\n\nfrom troupe.errors import LoadError, WriteRefused\nfrom troupe.location import Location, Url\nfrom troupe.paths import unwrap, walk\nfrom troupe.schema import Schema, Step\nfrom troupe.tree import Node, SequenceNode\nfrom troupe.values import Value\n\nFILE_PREFIX: Final = \"file:\"\nREMOTE_PREFIXES: Final = (\"https://\", \"http://\")\nYAML_SUFFIXES: Final = (\".yml\", \".yaml\")\n_HTTP_SCHEMES: Final = (\"http\", \"https\")\n\ntype IncludeKind = Literal[\"file\", \"remote\"]\n\n\ndef include_kind(text: str) -> IncludeKind | None:\n    \"\"\"Whether ``text`` looks like an include, and which kind (contracts/cli.md, S-1).\n\n    A case-sensitive prefix test, as ``file:`` has always been tested: ``file:...`` is a file\n    include, ``https://...`` or ``http://...`` a remote one; anything else (``HTTPS://x``,\n    ``ftp://x``, `` file:x``) is not an include.\n    \"\"\"\n    if text.startswith(FILE_PREFIX):\n        return \"file\"\n    if text.startswith(REMOTE_PREFIXES):\...[truncated 6785 chars]
tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"The include rule in ``troupe.includes`` (spec S-1, S-2, S-3, S-7, S-10..S-13; research R-3,\nR-8): include kind, target resolution, YAML-or-text, the literal rule, PATH step kinds and the\nVALUE marking that ``set`` uses.\n\nTrees for ``path_steps`` are real files loaded through ``loader.load``; schemas are real files\nloaded through ``load_schema``.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport subprocess\nimport sys\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom troupe import loader\nfrom troupe.errors import LoadError, PathError, WriteRefused\nfrom troupe.includes import (\n    IncludeStr,\n    LiteralStr,\n    include_kind,\n    is_yaml_target,\n    mark_literals,\n    path_steps,\n    reads_as_include,\n    resolve_target,\n)\nfrom troupe.location import Url\nfrom troupe.schema import SCHEMA_FILE_NAME, Schema, load_schema\nfrom troupe.tree import Node\nfrom troupe.values import Value\n\nTEAM = Url(\"https://h/a/team.yaml\")\n\n\ndef _tree(tmp_path: Path, text: str) -> Node:\n    config = tmp_path / \"troupe.yml\"\n    config.write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return loader.load(config)\n\n\ndef _schema(tmp_path: Path, document: object) -> Schema:\n    state_dir = tmp_path / \".troupe\"\n    state_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n    (state_dir / SCHEMA_FILE_NAME).write_text(json.dumps(document), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    schema = load_schema(state_dir)\n    assert schema is not None\n    return schema\n\n\n# --- include_kind ----------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\n    (\"text\", \"kind\"),\n    [\n        (\"file:x\", \"file\"),\n        (\"https://x\", \"remote\"),\n        (\"http://x\", \"remote\"),\n        (\"HTTPS://x\", None),\n        (\"ftp://x\", None),\n        (\"files:x\", None),\n        (\"\", None),\n        (\" file:x\", None),\n    ],\n)\ndef test_include_kind_is_a_case_sensitive_prefix_test(text: str, kind: str | None) -> None:\n    assert include_kind(text) == kind\n...[truncated 10913 chars]

No existing module changes yet.\" && git log -1 --oneline && git status --short"}

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"Build the SourceTree from the root file plus recursive local and remote includes (FR-007, FR-008;\nspec S-1 to S-3, S-7, S-10).\n\nFiles are read fresh on every call (no caching across runs), as bytes decoded with strict UTF-8,\nnever in text mode, so CRLF and a BOM stay in ``SourceFile.text`` and parser indices match it\n(research R-05, R-14). Remote content is fetched by ``remote.fetch`` with the same decoding.\n\nInclude rule: decided by ``troupe.includes``, never here (TROUPE-007; research R-3). A string\nvalue of any style starting ``file:``, ``https://`` or ``http://`` is an include unless it is a\nliteral: tagged ``!literal``, or at a path the schema marks ``format: uri`` (S-7, S-10;\n``includes.reads_as_include``). ``file:`` names a target relative to the directory of the\n(resolved) local file containing it, or, inside remote content, relative to the containing URL\n(S-1; ``includes.resolve_target``); a ``file:`` that would leave http(s) there is refused. A\nremote string names its URL as written. A target whose path (for a URL: its URL path) ends\n``.yml``/``.yaml`` (case-sensitive) is a ``YamlInclude`` whose content is embedded in place; any\nother target is a ``TextInclude`` whose value is the target's exact text (S-2). Mapping keys are\nnever includes; a ``!literal`` key is refused. Everything reached through a remote include is\nremote (``SourceFile.remote``).\n\nFetching (S-2; research R-10): each URL is fetched at most once per load. Fetched texts are kept\nin the caller's ``fetched`` map, so every reload in one command reuses them; a failed fetch is a\n``LoadError`` naming the URL, the including location and the path (contracts/cli.md). Include\ncycles are detected on locations, URLs included.\n\nMapping form (FR-001 to FR-003): a mapping with a ``$ref`` key (any key quoting) is an include\nwhose include string is the ``$ref`` value; its other keys are *overrides*. A ``$ref`` key is\nnever data (spec D-1). With no overrides the in...[truncated 25740 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"The loader with remote includes, against a real local HTTP server (spec S-1 to S-3; R-3, R-10).\n\nEach test writes real files under ``tmp_path``, serves remote documents from the ``remote``\nfixture and loads with ``troupe.loader.load``; the server's request log shows what was fetched.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import LOCAL_NO_PROXY\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\nfrom troupe import loader\nfrom troupe.errors import LoadError\nfrom troupe.leaves import flatten\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.location import Url\nfrom troupe.paths import lookup, unwrap\nfrom troupe.tree import (\n    AliasNode,\n    MappingNode,\n    MergedMapping,\n    Node,\n    ScalarNode,\n    SourceFile,\n    TextInclude,\n    YamlInclude,\n)\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _bypass_proxy(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    # The test server is local; a proxy configured in the environment must not intercept it.\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"NO_PROXY\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"no_proxy\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n\n\ndef _config(tmp_path: Path, text: str, files: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> Path:\n    for rel, content in (files or {}).items():\n        target = tmp_path / rel\n        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        target.write_text(content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    config = tmp_path / \"troupe.yml\"\n    config.write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return config\n\n\ndef _at(root: Node, dotted: str) -> Node:\n    \"\"\"The node at ``dotted`` as found (includes and aliases not unwrapped at the end).\"\"\"\n    return lookup(root, dotted.split(\".\"), dotted)\n\n\ndef _paths(remote: RemoteServer) -> list[str]:\n    return [request.path for request in remote.requests()]\n\n\ndef _load_error(config: Path) -> LoadError:\n    with pytest.raises(LoadError) as caught:\n        load(config)\n    return caught.value\n\n\ndef test_remote_yaml_include_embeds_its_co...[truncated 12303 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"Literal strings in the loader: ``!literal`` and schema ``format: uri`` (spec S-3, S-7, S-10).\n\nEach test writes a real theatre state directory under ``tmp_path`` (configuration and, where\nneeded, ``troupe.schema.json`` read with ``schema.load_schema``) and loads it with\n``troupe.loader.load``. Include-looking strings point at the ``remote`` test server, so the\nrequest log proves that a literal is never fetched.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import LOCAL_NO_PROXY\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\nfrom troupe.errors import LoadError\nfrom troupe.leaves import flatten\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.paths import lookup, unwrap\nfrom troupe.schema import Schema, load_schema\nfrom troupe.tree import AliasNode, MappingNode, Node, ScalarNode, TextInclude, YamlInclude\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _bypass_proxy(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"NO_PROXY\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"no_proxy\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n\n\ndef _theatre(\n    tmp_path: Path, text: str, schema: object = None, files: dict[str, str] | None = None\n) -> tuple[Path, Schema | None]:\n    \"\"\"Write ``.troupe/troupe.yml`` (and the schema, if given); return it and the read schema.\"\"\"\n    state_dir = tmp_path / \".troupe\"\n    state_dir.mkdir()\n    for rel, content in (files or {}).items():\n        (state_dir / rel).write_text(content, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    if schema is not None:\n        (state_dir / \"troupe.schema.json\").write_text(json.dumps(schema), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    config = state_dir / \"troupe.yml\"\n    config.write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return config, load_schema(state_dir)\n\n\ndef _at(root: Node, dotted: str) -> Node:\n    return lookup(root, dotted.split(\".\"), dotted)\n\n\ndef _literal(root: Node, dotted: str) -> ScalarNode:\n    node = unwrap(_at(root, dotted))\n    assert isinstance(node, ScalarNode), node...[truncated 10279 chars]

tool: Bash {"command": "mkdir -p /tmp/wp05r && cat > /tmp/wp05r/probe.py <<'EOF'\nimport sys, pathlib\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.paths import walk, unwrap\nfrom troupe.values import parse_value\nfrom troupe.writeback.apply import apply_set\nfrom troupe.errors import WriteRefused, LoadError\ncases = {\n \"rebind_nested\": \"m:\\n  <<: {k: {r: &a 1}}\\n  k: {r: &a 2}\\nz: *a\\n\",\n \"rebind_list\": \"m:\\n  <<: [{k: {r: &a 1}}, {j: 0}]\\n  k: {r: &a 2}\\nz: *a\\n\",\n}\nfor name, text in cases.items():\n    d = pathlib.Path(\"/tmp/wp05r\")/name; d.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\n    f = d/\"c.yaml\"; f.write_text(text)\n    try:\n        root = load(f)\n    except LoadError as e:\n        print(name, \"LOADERR\", e); continue\n    for path, val in [([\"m\",\"k\"], \"{r: 3}\")]:\n        try:\n            plan = apply_set(root, path, \".\".join(path), parse_value(val), val)\n            print(name, \"WRITTEN:\", repr(list(plan.files.values())[0]) if plan.files else None)\n            if plan.tree is not None:\n                z = walk(plan.tree, [\"z\"]).nodes[-1]; print(\"  z now\", unwrap(z).value if hasattr(unwrap(z),'value') else unwrap(z))\n        except WriteRefused as e:\n            print(name, \"REFUSED:\", e)\nEOF\ncd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-e && uv run python /tmp/wp05r/probe.py"}

tool: Bash {"command": "mkdir -p /tmp/wp05r/lit && cat > /tmp/wp05r/lit.py <<'EOF'\nimport pathlib, json\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.schema import load_schema\nfrom troupe.paths import walk, unwrap\nfrom troupe.errors import LoadError\nd = pathlib.Path(\"/tmp/wp05r/lit\")\nsch = d/\"s.json\"; sch.write_text(json.dumps({\"properties\":{\"u\":{\"format\":\"uri\"},\"m\":{\"properties\":{\"k\":{\"format\":\"uri\"}}},\"l\":{\"items\":{\"format\":\"uri\"}}}}))\nschema = load_schema(sch) if True else None\ndef run(text, schema=None):\n    f = d/\"c.yaml\"; f.write_text(text)\n    try:\n        r = load(f, schema=schema)\n        from troupe.leaves import flatten\n        print(repr(text), \"->\", flatten(r))\n        return r\n    except LoadError as e:\n        print(repr(text), \"ERR\", e.message)\nrun(\"a: !literal\\nb: 1\\n\")\nrun(\"a: !literal 5\\n\")\nrun(\"a: !literal ''\\n\")\nrun(\"a: !literal |\\n  file:x\\n\")\nrun(\"u: &u https://127.0.0.1:1/x.yaml\\nv: *u\\n\", schema)\nrun(\"u: &u https://127.0.0.1:1/x.yaml\\nv: *u\\n\")\nrun(\"m:\\n  <<: {k: https://127.0.0.1:1/x}\\n\", schema)\nrun(\"l: [https://127.0.0.1:1/x, file:nothere]\\n\", schema)\nrun(\"x: {$ref: !literal file:x}\\n\")\nrun(\"x: {$ref: 5}\\n\")\nrun(\"x: {$ref: https://127.0.0.1:1/x.yaml}\\n\", schema)\nrun(\"u: {$ref: https://127.0.0.1:1/x.yaml}\\n\", schema)\nrun(\"x: {!literal k: v}\\n\")\nrun(\"a: &a !literal file:x\\nb: *a\\n\")\nrun(\"a: !literal &a file:x\\nb: *a\\n\")\nrun(\"k: [!literal https://127.0.0.1:1/x]\\n\")\nEOF\ncd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-e && NO_PROXY=127.0.0.1 uv run python /tmp/wp05r/lit.py 2>&1 | cut -c1-300"}

tool: Bash {"command": "cat > /tmp/sk/feedback-WP05.md <<'EOF'\n# WP05 review feedback (cycle 1)\n\n**Verdict: changes requested**\n\n1. **The inline `<<` remount lets a step-6 `set` silently change a value outside PATH (data safety, TROUPE-002).**\n   - **Where:** `src/troupe/loader.py`, in `_entries` (the `_is_merge_key(raw_key)` branch calling `_convert(raw_value, file, steps, merge_source=True)`) and the `steps if merge_source else ...` line in `_content`.\n   - **Problem:** Anchors inside an inline `<<` value now get the holding mapping's path as `Anchor.path` instead of `holder.<<[.i]...`. `writeback/apply.py` `_same_alias` uses `_anchor_path` (via `_same_outside`, R-8 rule 1) to tell apart two anchored nodes with the same name, and anchor redefinition is allowed. So an inline merge-source anchor and a written anchor of the same name can now share one path, and the verifier no longer sees an alias rebinding.\n   - **Failing scenario:**\n     ```yaml\n     m:\n       <<: {k: {r: &a 1}}\n       k: {r: &a 2}\n     z: *a\n     ```\n     Run `apply_set(root, [\"m\",\"k\"], \"m.k\", parse_value(\"{r: 3}\"), \"{r: 3}\")`.\n     - Base and `672f1f0`: refused (`m.k: cannot write this change byte-surgically; no file changed`).\n     - HEAD `d963d6a`: the plan is accepted and writes `k:\\n    r: 3`, and `z` silently changes from 2 to 1.\n     - The list form `<<: [{k: {r: &a 1}}, {j: 0}]` behaves the same.\n     - Reproduction: `/tmp/wp05r/probe.py`.\n   - **Required:**\n     - Keep the S-7 schema lookup (`_uri` / `_holds_include`) at the merge mount point.\n     - Keep `Anchor.path` (anchor identity) as before for merge-source content (`holder.<<[.i]...`). For example, thread a separate identity path next to the schema steps.\n     - Add a regression test: this exact case is refused with exit 3 and no file changed, at the `apply_set` level, which is the only place the verifier is reachable before WP08. Put it in `tests/unit/test_loader_literals.py` or `tests/unit/test_loader_remot...[truncated 841 chars]

Commit (\"fix(WP05): ...\", src/tests only, __pycache__ removed), mark-status T019..T023 done, no move-task. assistant: The review rejection is fixed in commit `e3d798c`. Both scenarios from `/tmp/wp05r/probe.py` are refused again with `m.k: cannot write this change byte-surgically; no file changed`, and all requested gates pass.\n\n**What changed** (`src/troupe/loader.py`)\n- The loader now passes a small frozen `_At` object instead of one `steps` list. It carries two paths:\n  - `segments` is the path as written, with `<<` and merge-list indexes kept (`holder.<<[.i]...`), as before WP05. Error messages and `Anchor.path` (the anchor identity the verifier compares) use it.\n  - `steps` is the schema path. Inside a `<<` value it stays at the holding mapping, so the S-7 lookup and the C3 check are unchanged.\n- The module docstring now describes the two paths.\n\n**Regression test** (`tests/unit/test_loader_literals.py`)\n- `test_anchor_in_an_inline_merge_source_keeps_its_written_path_as_identity` covers both the mapping form and the list form of `<<`.\n- It checks that the merge-source anchor's path is `("m","<<","k","r")` (or `("m","<<","0","k","r")` for the list) and the written one is `("m","k","r")`.\n- It runs `apply_set(root, ["m","k"], "m.k", {r: 3})` and expects `WriteRefused` with exit 3, the exact message above, and the file unchanged.\n- I ran it against the rejected loader from `d963d6a`: both cases fail there and pass with the fix.\n- `test_inline_merge_source_strings_are_judged_at_the_merged_path` still passes.\n\n**Gates**\n- `uv run pytest -q tests/unit tests/integration/test_aliases_acceptance.py tests/integration/test_set_aliases.py tests/integration/test_set_expand_alias.py`: 1809 passed in 173 s.\n- `uv run mypy --strict`: no issues in 93 files.\n- `uv run ruff check`: all checks passed.\n- `uv run ruff format --check`: 97 files already formatted.\n\n**Commit and status:** the commit touches only `src/troupe/loader.py` and `tests/unit/test_loader_literals.py`, with `__pycache__` removed first, and the worktree is clean. T019–T023 are marked done again; I did not run move-task.\n\nFrom before this cycle: the type-only edits in four non-owned test files (`672f1f0`) still need your sign-off. The full suite last passed on `d963d6a` (2376 tests); I have not re-run it after this fix, only the subset you asked for.

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"The one place that decides \"the configuration is loaded and valid\" (research R-10, TROUPE-007).\n\n``load_configuration`` is the single entry point every session uses. Its order is fixed:\n\n1. ``schema.load_schema(state_dir)``: the schema is read *first*, because it decides which\n   strings are literals (``format: uri``, spec S-7) and so what the loader fetches or reads as\n   an include. An unreadable or invalid schema is a ``LoadError`` naming the schema file (S-8).\n2. ``loader.load(config_file, overrides, schema=..., fetched=...)``: local files are read and\n   remote includes fetched, each URL at most once per command (``fetched`` is shared, R-10).\n3. Validation: when a schema exists, the resolved configuration (``to_data``) must match it,\n   else ``LoadError(\"configuration does not match schema <file> at <path>: <message>\")``.\n\nEvery failure here is a ``LoadError`` (exit 4). ``set`` needs exit 3 for a result that would not\nmatch the schema (S-9); it gets that by calling ``violation`` itself on its in-memory reload and\nraising its own ``WriteRefused``, rather than by catching the message produced here.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom collections.abc import Mapping\nfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n\nfrom troupe.errors import LoadError\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.location import Location, Url\nfrom troupe.paths import unwrap\nfrom troupe.schema import Schema, Violation, load_schema\nfrom troupe.theatre import Theatre\nfrom troupe.tree import MappingNode, Node, ScalarNode, SequenceNode, TextInclude\n\ntype Data = str | int | float | bool | None | dict[str, Data] | list[Data]\n\n\n@dataclass(frozen=True)\nclass Loaded:\n    \"\"\"A configuration that loaded and matches its schema (if any).\n\n    Attributes:\n        tree: The SourceTree of the configuration.\n        schema: The theatre's schema, or ``None`` when ``troupe.schema.json`` does not exist.\n        fetched: The remote texts this command has fetched, by URL. The...[truncated 2905 chars]

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-f && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='src/troupe/commands/watch.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nold='''    to ``None``, since ``locate`` has just found it absent. tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"``troupe.configuration``: the one \"loaded and valid\" entry point (research R-10, spec S-8).\n\nEach test writes a real theatre under ``tmp_path`` (configuration, include files and, where\nneeded, ``troupe.schema.json``) and loads it with ``load_configuration``. Remote includes come\nfrom the ``remote`` test server, whose request log shows what was fetched.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport math\nimport warnings\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom ruamel.yaml import YAML\n\nfrom conftest import LOCAL_NO_PROXY\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\nfrom troupe import yamlout\nfrom troupe.configuration import Loaded, load_configuration, to_data, violation\nfrom troupe.errors import FetchError, LoadError\nfrom troupe.location import Url\nfrom troupe.schema import load_schema\nfrom troupe.theatre import Theatre, locate\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _bypass_proxy(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"NO_PROXY\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"no_proxy\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n\n\ndef _theatre(tmp_path: Path, files: dict[str, str], schema: object = None) -> Theatre:\n    \"\"\"Write ``.troupe/<rel>`` for every file (and the schema, if given); locate the theatre.\"\"\"\n    state_dir = tmp_path / \".troupe\"\n    for rel, content in files.items():\n        target = state_dir / rel\n        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n        target.write_bytes(content.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    if schema is not None:\n        (state_dir / \"troupe.schema.json\").write_text(json.dumps(schema), encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return locate(tmp_path)\n\n\ndef _shown(loaded: Loaded) -> object:\n    \"\"\"What ``show`` prints for the whole configuration, read back by a YAML 1.2 reader.\"\"\"\n    reader = YAML(typ=\"safe\", pure=True)\n    with warnings.catch_warnings():\n        warnings.simplefilter(\"ignore\")\n        return reader.load(yamlout.to_text(yamlout.render(loaded.tree), None))\n\n\n# --- to_...[truncated 9340 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"Rendering ``!literal`` (spec S-10, S-11, S-13; FR-011; research R-4, R-8).\n\nNew string values are rendered by ``scalars.render_scalar`` and ``blocks.render_new_multiline``;\nboth write ``!literal `` before an ``includes.LiteralStr``. Every rendered text is read back with\n``yamlcore.compose(..., allow_anchors=True)`` (file mode) to show it carries the tag and the\nexact value. Whole-file edits are re-loaded through ``troupe.loader.load``.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import MakeTheatre\nfrom troupe import yamlcore\nfrom troupe.includes import IncludeStr, LiteralStr\nfrom troupe.leaves import flatten\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.paths import parse_path, walk\nfrom troupe.tree import AliasNode, MappingNode, Node, ScalarNode, SequenceNode, YamlInclude\nfrom troupe.writeback import apply_edits\nfrom troupe.writeback.aliases import expand_edits\nfrom troupe.writeback.blocks import render_new_multiline\nfrom troupe.writeback.collections import (\n    Value,\n    convert_include_edits,\n    flow_append_edits,\n    new_item_edits,\n    new_key_edits,\n    render_flow_value,\n    replace_value_edits,\n)\nfrom troupe.writeback.placement import concrete_parent\nfrom troupe.writeback.scalars import LITERAL_PREFIX, Context, render_scalar, render_string\nfrom troupe.yamlcore import RawMapping, RawNode, RawScalar, RawSequence\n\nVIRTUAL = Path(\"/virtual/troupe.yml\")\n\n\ndef _compose(text: str) -> RawNode:\n    root = yamlcore.compose(text, \"<test>\", allow_anchors=True)\n    assert root is not None\n    return root\n\n\ndef _read_back(rendered: str, context: Context) -> RawScalar:\n    \"\"\"Compose ``rendered`` as a mapping value (block) or a list item (flow).\"\"\"\n    root = _compose(f\"k: {rendered}\\n\" if context == \"block\" else f\"[{rendered}]\\n\")\n    if isinstance(root, RawMapping):\n        node = root.pairs[0][1]\n    else:\n        assert isinstance(root, RawSequence)\n        node =...[truncated 15935 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"User Story 1: read configuration that includes shared settings from a URL (real CLI, real HTTP).\n\nRemote content is served by the ``remote`` test server on 127.0.0.1 (spec C-006); its request\nlog shows what each command fetched. Every test asserts the exit code, stdout exactly and\nstderr (empty, or the single ``troupe: ...`` line). The number in a test's comment is the US1\nacceptance scenario it covers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport socket\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import (\n    HISTORY_PREFIX,\n    MakeTheatre,\n    WatchProcess,\n    all_files,\n    core,\n    history_events,\n    run_cli,\n    snapshot,\n)\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer, Route\n\n\ndef _cli(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    return result.code, result.out, result.err\n\n\ndef _root(theatre: Path) -> Path:\n    return (theatre / \".troupe\" / \"troupe.yml\").resolve()\n\n\ndef _paths(remote: RemoteServer) -> list[str]:\n    return [request.path for request in remote.requests()]\n\n\ndef _closed_port_url() -> str:\n    \"\"\"An http URL on 127.0.0.1 whose port was just released, so nothing listens there.\"\"\"\n    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as probe:\n        probe.bind((\"127.0.0.1\", 0))\n        port = probe.getsockname()[1]\n    return f\"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/team.yaml\"\n\n\ndef test_get_reads_a_value_from_a_remote_yaml_include(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, remote: RemoteServer\n) -> None:  # 1\n    url = remote.serve(\"/team.yaml\", \"model: opus\\n\")\n    theatre = make_theatre({\"troupe.yml\": f\"team: {url}\\n\"})\n\n    assert _cli(theatre, \"get\", \"team.model\") == (0, \"opus\\n\", \"\")\n    assert [(r.method, r.path) for r in remote.requests()] == [(\"GET\", \"/team.yaml\")]\n\n\ndef test_get_of_a_remote_text_include_prints_the_body_exactly(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, remote: RemoteServer\n) -> None...[truncated 9928 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"User Story 4: URL settings stay strings (real CLI, real HTTP; spec S-7, S-10, S-3).\n\nA string is a literal when tagged ``!literal`` or when the schema, walked through\n``properties``, ``additionalProperties`` and ``items`` only, marks its path ``\"format\": \"uri\"``.\nInclude-looking strings point at the ``remote`` test server, whose request log proves that a\nliteral is never fetched, and that a string the walk does not reach is an include. The number\nin a test's comment is the US4 acceptance scenario it covers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import MakeTheatre, run_cli\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\n\n\ndef _cli(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    return result.code, result.out, result.err\n\n\ndef _schema(document: object) -> str:\n    return json.dumps(document, indent=2) + \"\\n\"\n\n\ndef test_schema_uri_setting_is_a_literal_and_is_not_fetched(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, remote: RemoteServer\n) -> None:  # 1\n    url = remote.serve(\"/notify\", \"a body nobody should read\\n\")\n    theatre = make_theatre(\n        {\n            \"troupe.yml\": f\"webhook: {url}\\n\",\n            \"troupe.schema.json\": _schema({\"properties\": {\"webhook\": {\"format\": \"uri\"}}}),\n        }\n    )\n\n    assert _cli(theatre, \"get\", \"webhook\") == (0, f\"{url}\\n\", \"\")\n    assert _cli(theatre, \"show\") == (0, f\"webhook: {url}\\n\", \"\")\n    assert remote.requests() == []\n\n\ndef test_uri_through_additional_properties_and_items_is_literal(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, remote: RemoteServer\n) -> None:  # 2\n    first, second = remote.url(\"/a.yaml\"), remote.url(\"/b.md\")\n    schema = {\n        \"properties\": {\n            \"agents\": {\n                \"additionalProperties\": {\n                    \"properties\": {\"urls\": {\"items\": {\"format\": \"uri\"}}},\n           ...[truncated 4956 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"User Story 5, load part: the configuration matches its schema (real CLI; spec S-8, FR-009).\n\nWhen ``.troupe/troupe.schema.json`` exists, every command that loads the configuration validates\nthe resolved configuration (remote content included) against it as JSON Schema 2020-12 and exits\n4 naming the schema file and the first failing path; an unreadable or invalid schema exits 4\nnaming the schema file. The load happens before the history is opened, so a failure records\nnothing. Scenario 3 (``set``) belongs to the ``set`` tests. The number in a test's comment is the\nUS5 acceptance scenario it covers.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\nfrom test_show_sources import CONTRACT_FILES, CONTRACT_OUTPUT\n\nfrom conftest import HISTORY_PREFIX, MakeTheatre, all_files, core, history_events, run_cli, snapshot\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\n\nMODEL_IS_A_STRING = {\"properties\": {\"model\": {\"type\": \"string\"}}}\nLOADING_COMMANDS = {\n    \"get\": (\"get\", \"name\"),\n    \"show\": (\"show\",),\n    \"set\": (\"set\", \"name\", \"other\"),\n    \"history\": (\"history\",),\n}\n\n\ndef _cli(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    return result.code, result.out, result.err\n\n\ndef _schema(document: object) -> str:\n    return json.dumps(document, indent=2) + \"\\n\"\n\n\ndef _schema_file(theatre: Path) -> Path:\n    return (theatre / \".troupe\" / \"troupe.schema.json\").resolve()\n\n\ndef _has_history(theatre: Path) -> bool:\n    return any(rel.startswith(HISTORY_PREFIX) for rel in all_files(theatre))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"command\", LOADING_COMMANDS.values(), ids=LOADING_COMMANDS.keys())\ndef test_configuration_not_matching_the_schema_exits_4_naming_schema_and_path(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, command: tuple[str, ...]\n) -> None:  # 1 (a local value)\n    theatre = make_theatre(\n        {\"troupe.yml\": \"name...[truncated 7043 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"``watch`` with a schema and remote includes (spec S-6, FR-006; research R-11), real processes.\n\nThe schema file is in ``watch``'s baseline, because it decides what is a literal and what is\nvalid: editing it triggers a reconcile like any watched file. URLs are not in the baseline:\n``watch`` never polls them, and fetches remote content again only when a reconcile runs. Waits\nfollow ``test_watch.py``: ``recorded_within`` (2 s, FR-011) and ``QUIET`` (nothing happens).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import MakeTheatre, WatchProcess, core, history_events, recorded_within\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\n\ntype Event = dict[str, object]\n\nQUIET = 2.5  # seconds: longer than the 2 s contract, so \"nothing happened\" means nothing will\nURI_MARK = {\"properties\": {\"webhook\": {\"format\": \"uri\"}}}\nSERVED = \"served text\\n\"\n\n\ndef _write(theatre: Path, rel: str, text: str) -> float:\n    \"\"\"Hand-edit ``.troupe/<rel>`` and return ``time.monotonic()`` right after the write.\"\"\"\n    (theatre / \".troupe\" / rel).write_bytes(text.encode(\"utf-8\"))\n    return time.monotonic()\n\n\ndef _set(seq: int, path: str, old: object, new: object) -> Event:\n    return {\"seq\": seq, \"op\": \"set\", \"path\": path, \"old\": old, \"new\": new}\n\n\ndef _watch(theatre: Path) -> WatchProcess:\n    return WatchProcess(\"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n\n\n@pytest.mark.parametrize(\"change\", [\"mark-added\", \"mark-removed\", \"schema-deleted\"])\ndef test_editing_the_schema_triggers_a_reconcile(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, remote: RemoteServer, change: str\n) -> None:\n    url = remote.serve(\"/notify\", SERVED)\n    marked = change != \"mark-added\"\n    files: dict[str, str | bytes] = {\"troupe.yml\": f\"a: 1\\nwebhook: {url}\\n\"}\n    files[\"troupe.schema.json\"] = json.dumps(URI_MARK if marked else {\"type\": \"object\"})\n    theatre = make_theatre(files)\n    imported = his...[truncated 4549 chars]

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-g && cat > /tmp/wp07probe.py <<'EOF'\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.writeback import apply_edits\nfrom troupe.writeback.collections import replace_value_edits, new_key_edits, render_flow_value\nfrom troupe.includes import LiteralStr\nfrom troupe import yamlcore\nV=Path(\"/virtual/troupe.yml\")\ndef t(before, key, new):\n    root=load(V, overrides={V:before})\n    old=root.get(key).value\n    try:\n        out=apply_edits(before, replace_value_edits(root.origin.file, root, key, old, new, 2, path=key))\n    except Exception as e:\n        out=f\"ERR {type(e).__name__}: {e}\"\n    print(repr(before), repr(new), \"->\", repr(out))\nt(\"u: !literal\\nz: 0\\n\",\"u\",\"hello\")\nt(\"u: !literal\\nz: 0\\n\",\"u\",LiteralStr(\"https://y\"))\nt(\"u: !literal |\\n  a  \\n\",\"u\",\"x\\n y \\n\\n\\n\")\nt(\"u: !literal https://x\\n\",\"u\",LiteralStr(\"a\\nb\\n\"))\nt(\"u: !literal &a https://x\\nz: *a\\n\",\"u\",{\"k\":LiteralStr(\"file:x\")})\nt(\"f: [!literal # c\\n  https://x]\\n\",\"f\",None) if False else None\nfor v in [LiteralStr(\"a\\nb\"), LiteralStr(\"x # y\"), LiteralStr(\"\"), LiteralStr(\"'q\"), LiteralStr(\"  lead\")]:\n    r=render_flow_value(v); n=yamlcore.compose(f\"[{r}]\\n\",\"<t>\",allow_anchors=True).items[0]\n    print(repr(r), repr(n.value), n.tag is not None)\nEOF\nuv run python /tmp/wp07probe.py; git stash list >/dev/null"}

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-g && cat > /tmp/wp07p2.py <<'EOF'\nfrom troupe.writeback.collections import render_flow_value\nfrom troupe.writeback.blocks import render_new_multiline\nfrom troupe.writeback.scalars import render_scalar\nfrom troupe.includes import LiteralStr, IncludeStr\nfrom troupe import yamlcore\nfor v in [LiteralStr(\"a\\nb\"), LiteralStr(\"x # y\"), LiteralStr(\"\"), LiteralStr(\"'q\"), LiteralStr(\"  lead\"), LiteralStr(\"true\"), LiteralStr(\"-\"), IncludeStr(\"file:x\")]:\n    r=render_flow_value(v); n=yamlcore.compose(f\"[{r}]\\n\",\"<t>\",allow_anchors=True).items[0]\n    b=render_scalar(v,None,\"block\") if \"\\n\" not in v else None\n    nb=yamlcore.compose(f\"k: {b}\\n\",\"<t>\",allow_anchors=True).pairs[0][1] if b is not None else None\n    print(repr(r), vars(n).get('text', n), '|', repr(b), nb)\nh,body=render_new_multiline(LiteralStr(\"a\\n b\\n\"),\"block\",2,2); print(h,body)\nEOF\nuv run python /tmp/wp07p2.py"}

One message builder,\n``remote_refusal``, names the outermost remote include on the route: the first one met from the\nroot, whose include string is written in a local file. It is used at two checkpoints:\n\n1. Here, in ``place``, right after the D-6 ``$ref`` refusal and ``walk``, and **before** the alias\n   decision: an existing PATH whose value comes from remote content (a scalar or collection in a\n   remote file, a remote YAML or text include, a merged view with a remote side) is refused, so a\n   value reached through an alias inside remote content reports the include line to change, not\n   ``--expand-alias``. tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='src/troupe/writeback/apply.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ndef rep(old,new,cnt=1):\n    global s\n    assert s.count(old)==cnt,(old,s.count(old))\n    s=s.replace(old,new)\nrep('''import re\nfrom collections.abc import Callable, Mapping\n''','''import re\nfrom collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Mapping\n''')\nrep('''    ReplaceText,\n    place,\n)''','''    ReplaceText,\n    place,\n    remote_refusal,\n)''')\nrep('''    file, edits = _edits(placement, dotted, value)\n    new_text = apply_edits(file.text, edits)\n    if new_text == file.text:\n        _refuse(dotted)\n    files = {file: new_text}\n    tree = _verify(root, segments, dotted, files, lambda node: _matches(node, value))\n    return WritePlan(files, tree)\n''','''    file, edits = _edits(placement, dotted, value)\n    _refuse_remote(root, segments, dotted, [file])\n    new_text = apply_edits(file.text, edits)\n    if new_text == file.text:\n        _refuse(dotted)\n    files = {file: new_text}\n    tree = _verify(root, segments, dotted, files, lambda node: _matches(node, value))\n    return WritePlan(files, tree)\n\n\ndef _refuse_remote(root: Node, segments: list[str], dotted: str, files: Iterable[SourceFile]) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Checkpoint 2 of the remote rule (S-4, R-7): no plan may write remote content.\n\n    Checkpoint 1 (``placement.place``) refused existing values from remote content; this one sees\n    the files a plan actually writes, so it also catches a list append or a key inside an item of\n    a remote list, ``--in-include`` into remote content and an ``--expand-alias`` copy there.\n    ``fileio.write_files`` refuses a remote source once more, as the last net.\n\n    Raises:\n        WriteRefused: Naming the outermost remote include on PATH's route (``remote_refusal``).\n    \"\"\"\n    for file in files:\n        if file.remote:\n            raise remote_refusal(walk(root, segments), ...[truncated 919 chars]
tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='src/troupe/writeback/placement.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nold='''            from remote content (S-4, message from ``remote_refusal``), is reached through an alias without\n            ``expand_alias`` or through a merge key ``<<`` (R-6), names a mapping holding ``<<``\n            or would create a key ``<<`` (A-0), the whole value at PATH comes from a YAML\n            include, or PATH names a mapping both an include and an override define (D-10);\n            exit 3.'''\nnew='''            from remote content (S-4, message from ``remote_refusal``), is reached through an\n            alias without ``expand_alias`` or through a merge key ``<<`` (R-6), names a mapping\n            holding ``<<`` or would create a key ``<<`` (A-0), the whole value at PATH comes\n            from a YAML include, or PATH names a mapping both an include and an override define\n            (D-10); exit 3.'''\nassert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new); open(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nuv run ruff format src/troupe/writeback/ && uv run ruff check src/troupe/writeback/ && uv run mypy --strict src/troupe/writeback/placement.py src/troupe/writeback/apply.py", "description": "Fix long docstring line and recheck"}
tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h && NO_PROXY=127.0.0.1 PYTHONPATH=tests uv run python -c \"\nimport tempfile, pathlib\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.writeback.placement import place\nfrom troupe.writeback.apply import apply_set\nfrom troupe.errors import WriteRefused\ns=RemoteServer()\ninner=s.serve('/inner.yaml','deep: 1\\n')\nu=s.serve('/team.yaml','model: opus\\ntools: [a, b]\\nd: &x {b: 1}\\nc: *x\\nnest: '+inner+'\\n')\nc=s.serve('/coder.yaml','temperature: 0.7\\nmodel: gpt\\n')\nd=pathlib.Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()); f=d/'troupe.yml'\nf.write_text(f'team: \\\"{u}\\\"  # shared\\ncoder:\\n  \\$ref: {c}\\n  temperature: 0.3\\n')\nr=load(f)\nfor p in ['team.model','team','coder','coder.model','team.c.b','team.nest.deep','team.tools.0']:\n    try: print(p, place(r,p.split('.'),p))\n    except WriteRefused as e: print(p,'REFUSED',e.message)\nfor p,v in [('team.tools.2','x'),('team.newkey','1')]:\n    try: print(p, apply_set(r,p.split('.'),p,v,v,in_include=True).files)\n    except WriteRefused as e: print(p,'REFUSED',e.message)\nprint(place(r,['team','new'],'team.new'))\ns.close()\n\" 2>&1 | cut -c1-250", "description": "Smoke-test checkpoint 1 and 2 against a local server"}

spec S-4, S-9, S-11 to S-13; research R-7 to\nR-10):\n\n- Remote content is read-only: ``placement.place`` refuses an existing value from it before the\n  alias rule (checkpoint 1) and ``_refuse_remote`` refuses any plan that would write a remote\n  source (checkpoint 2); both use ``placement.remote_refusal``.\n- After ``place``, ``includes.mark_literals`` marks VALUE's include-looking strings:\n  ``LiteralStr`` (written ``!literal``) unless the schema marks the path ``format: uri``, or\n  ``IncludeStr`` (written untagged, an include) with ``include`` (``set --include``).\n- An existing scalar is replaced by a plain ``str`` and its tag is a separate decision\n  (``_scalar_edits``, ``_retag``): added for a ``LiteralStr``, kept for any other string but an\n  ``IncludeStr``, removed for an ``IncludeStr`` or a non-string.\n- Reloads (``_reload``) use the command's schema and fetched remote texts (``SetContext``); tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"The read-only rule for remote content at both checkpoints (spec S-4, S-5; research R-7).\n\nCheckpoint 1 is ``placement.place`` (an existing value from remote content, before the alias\nrule); checkpoint 2 is ``apply.apply_set`` (a plan that would write a remote source). Trees are\nloaded with ``troupe.loader.load`` from real files, with remote documents served by the\n``remote`` fixture; the server's request log shows that nothing but ``GET`` is ever sent.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import LOCAL_NO_PROXY\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\nfrom troupe.errors import WriteRefused\nfrom troupe.loader import load\nfrom troupe.paths import parse_path\nfrom troupe.tree import Node\nfrom troupe.values import parse_value\nfrom troupe.writeback.apply import apply_set\nfrom troupe.writeback.placement import (\n    AddKeys,\n    ConvertInclude,\n    ReplaceScalar,\n    place,\n)\n\nDASH = \"—\"\n\n\n@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)\ndef _bypass_proxy(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:\n    # The test server is local; a proxy configured in the environment must not intercept it.\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"NO_PROXY\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n    monkeypatch.setenv(\"no_proxy\", LOCAL_NO_PROXY)\n\n\ndef _config(tmp_path: Path, text: str) -> Path:\n    config = tmp_path / \"troupe.yml\"\n    config.write_text(text, encoding=\"utf-8\")\n    return config\n\n\ndef _refusal(config: Path, written: str, line: int, dotted: str) -> str:\n    return (\n        f\"{dotted}: value comes from remote include {written} ({config.resolve()} line {line});\"\n        f\" troupe does not change remote content {DASH} change that include line instead;\"\n        \" no file changed\"\n    )\n\n\ndef _place_refused(root: Node, dotted: str, *, expand_alias: bool = False) -> str:\n    with pytest.raises(WriteRefused) as caught:\n        place(root, parse_path(dotted), dotted, expand_alias=expand_alias)\n    assert caught.value.exit_code == 3\...[truncated 8192 chars]
tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h && uv run pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/unit/test_placement_remote.py 2>&1 | tail -30", "description": "Run the new placement remote unit tests"}
tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"User Stories 2 and 3: remote content is read-only; new keys become local overrides (real CLI).\n\nRemote content is served by the ``remote`` test server on 127.0.0.1 (spec C-006). Refusals\nassert exit 3, empty stdout, exactly one stderr line naming PATH and containing the include\nstring exactly as written in the local file, every theatre file unchanged, no event recorded and\nonly ``GET`` requests at the server (NFR-001, NFR-002). Successful writes compare the whole root\nfile byte for byte (NFR-003). The comment number is the acceptance scenario covered.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom conftest import MakeTheatre, history_events, run_cli, snapshot\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\n\nDASH = \"—\"\n\nTEAM = \"model: opus\\ntools: [read, write]\\nbase: &b {model: opus}\\ncoder: *b\\n\"\n\n\ndef _set(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", \"set\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    return result.code, result.out, result.err\n\n\ndef _get(theatre: Path, path: str) -> str:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", \"get\", path, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    assert (result.code, result.err) == (0, \"\"), result.err\n    return result.out\n\n\ndef _root(theatre: Path) -> Path:\n    return theatre / \".troupe\" / \"troupe.yml\"\n\n\ndef _refusal(theatre: Path, dotted: str, written: str, line: int) -> str:\n    return (\n        f\"troupe: {dotted}: value comes from remote include {written}\"\n        f\" ({_root(theatre).resolve()} line {line}); troupe does not change remote content\"\n        f\" {DASH} change that include line instead; no file changed\\n\"\n    )\n\n\ndef _assert_refused(\n    theatre: Path, remote: RemoteServer, args: tuple[str, ...], dotted: str, written: str, line: int\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run ``set *args``; assert the remote refusal and that nothing changed anywhere.\"\"\"\n    events = history_events(theatre)  # imports the theatre first: the refusal records n...[truncated 5992 chars]
tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h && uv run pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/integration/test_remote_readonly.py 2>&1 | tail -30", "description": "Run the remote read-only integration tests"}
tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"``set`` with the schema, ``--include`` and ``!literal`` tags (US5-3; S-9, S-11 to S-13; R-8 to R-10).\n\nReal CLI, real files, the real event store and the ``remote`` test server (spec C-006). Refusals\nassert exit 3 (or 2 for a bad option), empty stdout, the one stderr line, every theatre file\nunchanged and no event recorded (NFR-002). Successful writes compare the whole root file byte for\nbyte (NFR-003) and read the value back with ``get``.\n\nThe tag tests cover each way an existing scalar is replaced (plain, quoted, block, flow and the\nkind-changing fallback) against the rule in research R-8: an untagged scalar gets ``!literal``\nfor a literal VALUE, a tagged one keeps it for any string and loses it for a number or an\n``--include`` include.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import MakeTheatre, history_events, run_cli, snapshot\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\n\nSCHEMA_FILE = \"troupe.schema.json\"\n\n\ndef _set(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> tuple[int, str, str]:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", \"set\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    return result.code, result.out, result.err\n\n\ndef _get(theatre: Path, path: str) -> str:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", \"get\", path, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    assert (result.code, result.err) == (0, \"\"), result.err\n    return result.out\n\n\ndef _root(theatre: Path) -> Path:\n    return theatre / \".troupe\" / \"troupe.yml\"\n\n\ndef _schema(properties: dict[str, object]) -> str:\n    return json.dumps({\"type\": \"object\", \"properties\": properties})\n\n\ndef _refused(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Run ``set *args``; assert exit 3 changing nothing and recording nothing; return stderr.\"\"\"\n    events = history_events(theatre)\n    before = snapshot(theatre)\n\n    code, out, err = _set(theatre, *args)\n\n    assert (code, out) == (3, \"\")\n    assert err.startswith(\"troupe: \") and err.count(\"\\n\") == ...[truncated 11338 chars]

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/integration/test_set_schema.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nold='''        (\"d: x  # c\\\\n\", (\"d\", \"https://x.example/y\"), \"d: !literal https://x.example/y  # c\\\\n\"),'''\nnew='''        # (the comment keeps its column, or gets one space once the value reaches it: FR-020)\n        (\"d: x  # c\\\\n\", (\"d\", \"https://x.example/y\"), \"d: !literal https://x.example/y # c\\\\n\"),\n        (\"d: x\" + \" \" * 30 + \"# c\\\\n\", (\"d\", \"file:a\"), \"d: !literal file:a\" + \" \" * 16 + \"# c\\\\n\"),'''\nassert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)\nold='''        (\"d: !literal https://old  # c\\\\n\", (\"d\", \"5\"), \"d: 5                       # c\\\\n\"),'''\nnew='''        (\"d: !literal https://old  # c\\\\n\", (\"d\", \"5\"), \"d: 5\" + \" \" * 21 + \"# c\\\\n\"),'''\nassert old in s; s=s.replace(old,new)\ns=s.replace('''        \"plain-gets-tag\",\n''','''        \"plain-gets-tag\",\n        \"plain-gets-tag-comment-column\",\n''')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nuv run ruff format tests && uv run ruff check tests && uv run pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/integration/test_set_schema.py 2>&1 | grep -E \"^(FAILED|E  )|passed|failed\" | head -20", "description": "Fix expected comment columns and rerun"}

tool: Bash {"command": "tail -n 3 ~/troupe-orch/logs/lane-h-pytest.log; cat ~/troupe-orch/logs/lane-h-static.log 2>/dev/null | head; cat > /tmp/sk/feedback-WP08.md <<'EOF'\n# WP08 review feedback (cycle 1)\n\n**Verdict: changes requested**\n\n1. **The remote check for planned writes runs too late, so renderer refusals answer first** (`src/troupe/writeback/apply.py:250-251`, in `apply_set`).\n   - **Problem:** `_refuse_remote(root, segments, dotted, [file])` runs only after `file, edits = _edits(placement, dotted, value)`. The renderers that `_edits` calls (`new_key_edits`, `new_item_edits`, `convert_include_edits`) have their own refusals. When the plan would write remote content, one of those can fire first. The command still exits 3 and changes no file, but the message lacks the outermost local include string, which S-4/FR-004 require. R-7 requires the contract message before \"the other step 1–6 refusals can claim the case\". Reproduced with the CLI:\n     - **(a)** Remote `t2.yaml` = `tools:\\n  - a\\nitems:\\n  - n: 1\\nnote: |\\n  text` (no trailing newline); local `t: http://127.0.0.1:PORT/t2.yaml`.\n       - Command: `set --in-include t.newk 2`.\n       - Output: `troupe: cannot write t.newk in http://…/t2.yaml: the file ends inside a block scalar without a final line break, …`\n     - **(b)** Remote `t3.yaml` = `x: >-\\n  http://127.0.0.1:PORT/inner.yaml\\n` (a nested remote include written as a folded block scalar); local `u: http://127.0.0.1:PORT/t3.yaml`.\n       - Command: `set u.x.newk 1`.\n       - Output: `troupe: u.x.newk: the include at http://…/t3.yaml line 1 spans several lines; cannot convert it …` (names the remote file, not the outermost local include).\n   - **Required:**\n     - Decide the file to be written from the placement itself, before rendering any edits:\n       - `ConvertInclude` → `include.origin.file`\n       - `AddKeys` → `mapping.origin.file`\n       - `AppendItem` → `sequence.origin.file`\n       - `ReplaceCollection` / `ReplaceScalar` → ...[truncated 1074 chars]

The renderers that `_edits` calls (`new_key_edits`, `new_item_edits`, `convert_include_edits`) have their own refusals. When the plan would write remote content, one of those can fire first. T034–T041 are marked done again, the WP was not moved, and the full 16-minute suite was not run.\n\n**What changed**\n- **`apply.py`:** a new `_planned_file(placement)` works out which file a placement will write, using the mapping you gave:\n  - `ConvertInclude` → `include.origin.file`\n  - `AddKeys` → `mapping.origin.file`\n  - `AppendItem` → `sequence.origin.file`\n  - `ReplaceCollection` / `ReplaceScalar` → `node.origin.file`\n- **Check order:** `apply_set` now calls `_refuse_remote` on that file before `_edits`, so no renderer refusal can answer first. The check after `_edits` stays as a second net. The text-include and `--expand-alias` paths already ran the check before rendering, so they are unchanged.\n- **Regression tests:** two CLI tests in `tests/integration/test_remote_readonly.py`, using the real server. Each asserts the exact contract message, no file change, no event and only `GET` requests:\n  - `test_in_include_into_remote_file_ending_inside_a_block_scalar_gets_the_remote_refusal` covers case (a).\n  - `test_new_key_under_a_multi_line_nested_remote_include_gets_the_remote_refusal` covers case (b); the message names the outer `t3.yaml` include in the local file.\n- With the `apply.py` change stashed, both new tests fail; with it, both pass.\n\n**Test counts**\n- 2107 passed across `tests/unit` plus `test_remote_readonly` (now 13 tests), `test_set_schema`, `test_set_existing`, `test_set_overrides`, `test_overrides_acceptance`, `test_set_aliases`, `test_set_expand_alias`, `test_set_new_entries` and `test_set_blocks`.\n- mypy --strict found no issues in 103 source files.\n\nFiles changed:\n- /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h/src/troupe/writeback/apply.py\n- /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-h/tests/integration/test_remote_readonly.py

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"User Story 6: ``set`` writes include-looking strings safely (S-11, S-12, S-13), real CLI.\n\nEvery success compares the bytes of every theatre file (history store excluded) with the whole\nexpected text, reads the value back with ``get`` and, where the write is the story, checks the\none recorded ``set`` event. Every refusal asserts its exit code, empty stdout, exactly one\n``troupe: `` stderr line, every file unchanged and no event recorded (NFR-002). The comment\nnumber is the US6 acceptance scenario covered.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import CliResult, MakeTheatre, core, history_events, run_cli, snapshot\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\n\ntype Files = dict[str, str | bytes]\n\nSCHEMA_FILE = \"troupe.schema.json\"\n\n\ndef _set(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> CliResult:\n    return run_cli(\"config\", \"set\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n\n\ndef _get(theatre: Path, path: str) -> str:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", \"get\", path, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    assert (result.code, result.err) == (0, \"\"), result.err\n    return result.out\n\n\ndef _bytes(files: Files) -> dict[str, bytes]:\n    return {\n        f\".troupe/{rel}\": content.encode(\"utf-8\") if isinstance(content, str) else content\n        for rel, content in files.items()\n    }\n\n\ndef _set_ok(theatre: Path, args: tuple[str, ...], expected: Files, path: str, shown: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Run ``set *args``; assert success, the whole theatre's bytes, and ``get path``.\"\"\"\n    result = _set(theatre, *args)\n    assert (result.code, result.out, result.err) == (0, \"\", \"\")\n    assert snapshot(theatre) == _bytes(expected)\n    assert _get(theatre, path) == shown\n\n\ndef _set_fails(theatre: Path, args: tuple[str, ...], code: int) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Run ``set *args``; assert ``code``, empty stdout, one stderr line, nothing changed.\"\"\"\n    events = history_events(theatre)  # imports the theatre f...[truncated 11087 chars]

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-i && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/integration/test_set_literal.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ni=s.index('def test_quoted_include_looking_value_keeps')\ns=s[:i]+'''@pytest.mark.parametrize(\n    (\"value\", \"line\", \"shown\"),\n    [\n        # a space alone needs no quoting in step 1, so none is added after the tag\n        (\"'file:my notes.md'\", \"notes: !literal file:my notes.md\\\\n\", \"file:my notes.md\\\\n\"),\n        # \" #\" would start a comment: step 1 single-quotes it, and so does the tagged form\n        (\"'file:my notes #1.md'\", \"notes: !literal 'file:my notes #1.md'\\\\n\", \"file:my notes #1.md\\\\n\"),\n        # \": \" would start a mapping\n        (\"'file:a: b'\", \"notes: !literal 'file:a: b'\\\\n\", \"file:a: b\\\\n\"),\n    ],\n    ids=[\"space-stays-plain\", \"hash-needs-quotes\", \"colon-needs-quotes\"],\n)\ndef test_quoted_include_looking_value_keeps_its_needed_quoting_after_the_tag(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, value: str, line: str, shown: str\n) -> None:  # S-11: \"!literal \" followed by the string exactly as step 1 would write it\n    theatre = make_theatre({\"troupe.yml\": \"a: 1\\\\n\"})\n\n    _set_ok(theatre, (\"notes\", value), {\"troupe.yml\": f\"a: 1\\\\n{line}\"}, \"notes\", shown)\n'''\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nuv run ruff format tests/integration/test_set_literal.py && timeout 600 uv run pytest -q tests/integration/test_set_literal.py -k quoted 2>&1 | tail -3"}

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-i && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/integration/test_set_literal.py'\ns=open(p).read()\nold='''    err = _set_fails(theatre, (\"b\", \"file:t.yaml\"), 3)\n\n    assert err.startswith(\"troupe: b: \")\n    assert \"t.yaml\" in err\n'''\nnew='''    target = (theatre / \".troupe\" / \"t.yaml\").resolve()\n\n    err = _set_fails(theatre, (\"b\", \"file:t.yaml\"), 3)\n\n    assert err == (\n        f\"troupe: b: value comes from include {target}; refusing to replace it\"\n        \" (change a path inside it instead); no file changed\\\\n\"\n    )\n'''\nassert old in s\ns=s.replace(old,new)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nrm -rf /tmp/wp09x"}
tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"``set --include`` with remote targets (S-12, S-9, S-4), through the real CLI.\n\nRemote content is served by the ``remote`` test server on 127.0.0.1 (spec C-006). Successes\ncompare every theatre file byte for byte (NFR-003) and read the new values back with ``get``;\nrefusals assert exit 3, empty stdout, one ``troupe: `` stderr line, every file unchanged, no event\nrecorded and only ``GET`` requests at the server (NFR-001, NFR-002).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom conftest import CliResult, MakeTheatre, core, history_events, run_cli, snapshot\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer\n\nDASH = \"—\"\nSCHEMA_FILE = \"troupe.schema.json\"\nTEAM = \"model: opus\\ntools: [read, write]\\n\"\n\n\ndef _set(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> CliResult:\n    return run_cli(\"config\", \"set\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n\n\ndef _get(theatre: Path, path: str) -> str:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", \"get\", path, \"--theatre\", str(theatre))\n    assert (result.code, result.err) == (0, \"\"), result.err\n    return result.out\n\n\ndef _root(theatre: Path) -> Path:\n    return theatre / \".troupe\" / \"troupe.yml\"\n\n\ndef _set_ok(theatre: Path, *args: str) -> None:\n    result = _set(theatre, *args)\n    assert (result.code, result.out, result.err) == (0, \"\", \"\")\n\n\ndef _set_fails(theatre: Path, remote: RemoteServer, *args: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Run ``set *args``; assert exit 3 changing and recording nothing; return stderr.\"\"\"\n    events = history_events(theatre)  # imports the theatre first: the refusal records nothing\n    before = snapshot(theatre)\n    remote.clear_log()\n\n    result = _set(theatre, *args)\n\n    assert (result.code, result.out) == (3, \"\")\n    assert result.err.startswith(\"troupe: \") and result.err.count(\"\\n\") == 1\n    assert snapshot(theatre) == before\n    assert history_events(theatre) == events\n    assert {request.method for request in remote.requests()} <= {\"GET\"}\n    return...[truncated 3806 chars]

tool: Write {"content": "\"\"\"Cross-cutting acceptance for remote includes and a schema (step 7; WP10, T045).\n\nThese tests cover what no single user story owns: the non-functional requirements NFR-001\n(remote content is never written), NFR-002 (refusals change nothing and record nothing) and\nNFR-003 (byte-surgical writes), the spec's Edge Cases list, and history/lineage with remote\nleaves (S-6). Everything runs through the real CLI subprocess against real files, the real\nevent store and a real HTTP server on 127.0.0.1 (``remote`` fixture, C-006).\n\n\"Records no event\" is checked against the real history store (``stored_records``), which needs\nno configuration load, so it also holds while the configuration cannot load (exit 4 cases).\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport time\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport pytest\n\nfrom conftest import (\n    MakeTheatre,\n    WatchProcess,\n    core,\n    history_events,\n    recorded_within,\n    run_cli,\n    snapshot,\n    stored_records,\n)\nfrom remote_server import RemoteServer, Route\n\nDASH = \"—\"\n\n# Remote team file: a list, and anchors, aliases and a merge key used internally (step 6).\nTEAM = (\n    \"model: opus\\n\"\n    \"tools: [read, write]\\n\"\n    \"base: &b\\n\"\n    \"  model: opus\\n\"\n    \"coder: *b\\n\"\n    \"reviewer:\\n\"\n    \"  <<: *b\\n\"\n    \"  temperature: 0.2\\n\"\n)\n\n\ndef _cli(theatre: Path, *args: str, env: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> tuple[int, str, str]:\n    result = run_cli(\"config\", *args, \"--theatre\", str(theatre), env=env)\n    return result.code, result.out, result.err\n\n\ndef _root(theatre: Path) -> Path:\n    return theatre / \".troupe\" / \"troupe.yml\"\n\n\ndef _remote_refusal(theatre: Path, dotted: str, written: str, line: int) -> str:\n    return (\n        f\"troupe: {dotted}: value comes from remote include {written}\"\n        f\" ({_root(theatre).resolve()} line {line}); troupe does not change remote content\"\n        f\" {DASH} change that include line instead;...[truncated 16429 chars]

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-j && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace('''            head + \"docs: !literal plain-text  # note\\\\n\",''','''            head + \"docs: !literal plain-text           # note\\\\n\",  # comment keeps its column''')\ns=s.replace('''            head + \"docs: !literal file:p.md  # note\\\\n\",''','''            head + \"docs: !literal file:p.md # note\\\\n\",  # value reaches it: one space''')\ns=s.replace('''            head + \"docs: file:p.md  # note\\\\n\",''','''            head + \"docs: file:p.md                     # note\\\\n\",''')\ns=s.replace('''\"tools:\\\\n- read\\\\n- write\\\\n\"''','''\"tools:\\\\n  - read\\\\n  - write\\\\n\"''')\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\ngrep -n \"note\\\\\\\\n\\|tools:\" tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py", "description": "Fix expected bytes per comment column rule"}
tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-j && python3 - <<'EOF'\np='tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py'\ns=open(p).read()\ns=s.replace(\"import json\\nimport time\\n\",\"import json\\nimport time\\nfrom collections.abc import Callable\\n\")\ns=s.replace('''REFUSALS = {''','''type Breaker = Callable[[Path, RemoteServer], None]\n\nREFUSALS: dict[str, tuple[tuple[str, ...], int, Breaker | None]] = {''')\ns=s.replace(\"    breaker: object,\\n\",\"    breaker: Breaker | None,\\n\")\ns=s.replace(\"    if callable(breaker):\\n\",\"    if breaker is not None:\\n\")\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nuv run ruff format tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py && uv run ruff check tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py && uv run mypy --strict tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py 2>&1 | tail -3; timeout 600 uv run pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py 2>&1 | tail -5", "description": "Type breaker, lint, typecheck, rerun", "timeout": 620000}

tool: Bash {"command": "cd /work/repo/.worktrees/troupe-config-remote-includes-01M4BMJ5-lane-j && cat >> tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py <<'EOF'\n\n\n# --- S-1: includes inside remote content resolve against the URL and are remote ---------------\n\n\ndef test_s1_file_include_in_remote_content_is_fetched_relative_to_the_url_and_is_read_only(\n    make_theatre: MakeTheatre, remote: RemoteServer\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"S-1, S-4: ``file:prompts/sys.md`` inside ``/team/team.yaml`` is fetched as\n    ``/team/prompts/sys.md``; what it brings in is remote, so a ``set`` on it exits 3 naming the\n    outermost include, the one written in the local file.\"\"\"\n    url = remote.serve(\"/team/team.yaml\", \"prompt: file:prompts/sys.md\\n\")\n    remote.serve(\"/team/prompts/sys.md\", \"Be brief.\\n\")\n    theatre = make_theatre({\"troupe.yml\": f\"team: {url}\\n\"})\n\n    assert _cli(theatre, \"get\", \"team.prompt\") == (0, \"Be brief.\\n\", \"\")\n    assert sorted(r.path for r in remote.requests()) == [\"/team/prompts/sys.md\", \"/team/team.yaml\"]\n    before = snapshot(theatre)\n    assert _cli(theatre, \"set\", \"team.prompt\", \"x\") == (\n        3,\n        \"\",\n        _remote_refusal(theatre, \"team.prompt\", url, 1),\n    )\n    assert snapshot(theatre) == before\n    assert _methods(remote) == {\"GET\"}\nEOF\nuv run ruff format tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py && uv run ruff check tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py && timeout 300 uv run pytest -q -p no:cacheprovider tests/integration/test_remote_acceptance.py -k s1 2>&1 | tail -15", "description": "Add S-1 acceptance test and run it", "timeout": 320000}

(Corrected after WP08/WP10.)
